# V′ MAIL — the tranche inbox/outbound ledger (E13)

**The law (E13, owner-ruled 2026-07-17; V-PRIME §1 L7):** every wave OPENS with an inbox sweep
of the four landing paths below; new mail gets a row here (status UNREAD) BEFORE any other work;
a wave may not CLOSE with UNREAD mail addressed to its scope. Mail is MARKED, never merely
remembered — relay-received is never terminal (D37): a letter counts only once rowed here.

Sweep paths (ours + the three counterparties):

- `docs/tranches/V/` + `docs/tranches/V/coordination/` — inbound letters landing in OUR tree
  (`GLASS-INBOUND-*`, `ATLAS-INBOUND-*`, keyframes replies)
- `../glass-ui/docs/tranches/BK/coordination/` — glass outbound / constellation packets
  (ACTIVE-TRANCHE path — glass moved BI→BJ→BK; sweep the LIVE tranche's coordination dir,
  not a pinned letter. The stale BI pin here cost 19 days on the 08-09 letters → I-28/I-29)
- `../keyframes-v-exec/docs/tranches/V/coordination/` — the keyframes exchange, THE DELIVERY PATH (their
  `<SENDER>-INBOUND-*` grammar; the tranche root is NOT a mail path)
- `../keyframes.js/docs/tranches/V/coordination/` — the SACRED checkout: **READ-ONLY / NEVER-DELIVER**
  (sweep it for inbound, never land a letter there. A letter left in a frozen checkout is not
  delivered — the path, not the content, was the defect; that pin cost O-8 and O-11 55 and 52 days
  undelivered → I-26)
- `../sci-report/atlas/docs/tranches/P/coordination/` — atlas relay (sci-report routes via atlas)

Status vocabulary: **UNREAD → READ → FOLDED** (content landed in an owning wave/row) and/or
**ACKED** (reply sent). W41's archival of an original never touches its row — the row is the
durable mark.

## Durability (E13 robustness — three layers, honest about what survives what)

1. **In-session cron — TORN DOWN by owner order 2026-07-17** ("kill any remaining crons"),
   after the formation-era `ba1d0311` (:23) and execution-era `4f129d93` (:41) each served
   their span. No cron is re-created unless the owner orders one. Mail vigilance now rests
   ENTIRELY on the wave Step-0 sweeps (V-PRIME §2 — every wave opens with the four-path
   sweep; no wave closes with UNREAD mail in scope) plus layers 2–3 below.
2. **This ledger** — the on-disk truth. Any session, including one born after a crash, resumes
   the mail state from here alone.
3. **User memory** (`feedback-mail-inbox-law.md`) — loads into every future value.js session and
   instructs the opening sweep. Crash/session-wall survival lives in layers 2+3, by design.

New producer facts, measured on keyframes.js's served page (headed, real GPU, glass 7.0.0) — fold into BL's O-56 G-1 disposition; no new letter.** Probe `docs/tranches/X/keyframes/evidence/W13U/d/probe-d1.mjs` + `sampler.js` (rAF sampler; reports ×2 dev + ×2 gh-pages). (1) **Blur is the producer's**: `morph.css` `.glass-dock[data-morphing] { filter: blur(calc(var(--dock-reveal-blur) * …)) }` (1.25 px peak) rasterizes the WHOLE dock, text included, on 31/31 morph frames of every expand and collapse. (2) **Text is scaled mid-morph**: `layers.css` stagger sets `scale: calc(0.82 + 0.18 * var(--child-reveal))` on every `.dock-layer.is-active > *`, and the root carries `will-change: transform` + the `--dock-size-scale` transform (35/94 expand frames). (3) **Two owners per property**: the stagger drives `opacity`/`scale` per frame from the spring while the producer's own control transitions (`.dock-trigger`/`.dock-select-trigger`/`.dock-dropdown-trigger` `transition: … scale …, opacity …`; `.dock-icon-button`; `.tap-squish`; `.dark-mode-toggle-button`) run CSSTransitions on the SAME properties — 8 element·property pairs per frame at 1440 after the consumer cure (12 before), every one on a producer control. (4) **Content-size changes snap**: a fit-content dock whose label changes (scene Cube → Amiga, 402 → 410 px) jumps with no size morph. (5) reka's `<span hidden>` beside a `DropdownMenu` trigger occupies a stagger `nth-child(… of *)` slot. At rest after a morph the text is pixel-identical to a no-morph render (0/61 280 px differ, dpr 2, ×4) — the blur is in-flight only. Honest-RED ids stand: `DOCK-MORPH-ROOT` (O-56) · `DOCK-SCROLL-MORPH` (O-55).

**Addendum-beside O-56 G-4 (2026-09-23, X.W12.a — Track A · X·V; `claude-opus-5-5`; E-3: the O-56 row above is untouched). The black ground, bisected on value.js's served page (glass 7.0.0) — fold into BL's O-56 G-4 disposition; no new letter.** CONSUMER cause, cured at value.js `9711abdb`: the atmosphere canvas was `absolute inset-0` inside the growing `.app-layout`, so on `/` (About pane) it measured 1440×7578 CSS px → a 2160×11368 backing store at the producer's 1.5 DPR cap, past WebGPU `maxTextureDimension2D` 8192; the swapchain failed every frame (11835 `GPUValidationError` in one headed real-GPU run) and the arrived canvas presented black — owner frames 1/2/4 read pure black outside the plates, never glass's maroon. Now viewport-fixed; `w12-ground.spec.ts` reads `gpuErrors=0`, backing ≤ 8192. PRODUCER residue (UIA-V-55 · UIA-V-192, relay only, no consumer copy): (1) `useAurora`/the GPU substrate does not clamp its backing store to the adapter's `maxTextureDimension2D` — any consumer that sizes the canvas past it gets a per-frame validation storm, not a clean `rendererStatus` error; (2) after a size-caused failure the runtime retries init every frame and never re-arms when the canvas later shrinks under the limit (390-light: `/palettes` 1208 px, `/atmosphere` 844 px stayed dead after one oversize on `/`); (3) `isArmed` stays true through that storm, so a consumer keyed on it presents a dead field (value.js now also gates on `rendererStatus.phase !== "error"`, UIA-V-191). Ask: clamp in the shared resize policy + one-shot latch to the CSS placeholder on a size failure + re-arm on a later in-limit resize. Evidence: `docs/tranches/X/execution/A/X-W12.md` §X.W12.a; `docs/tranches/X/audit/UI-AUDIT-value.md` UIA-V-1/55/192.
